### [16] TOOL_RESULT (9307 chars)
{"result":"{\"id\": \"page_qa_runbook\", \"space_id\": \"space_ENG\", \"parent_id\": \"page_eng_home\", \"title\": \"QA Regression Runbook\", \"body\": \"<p>Manual QA regression pass run against every release candidate before we submit a build for review. Owned by QA (Vincent Parker); anyone cutting a build can run it, but a green matrix here is a gate on the <ac:link><ri:page ri:space-key=\\\"OPS\\\" ri:content-title=\\\"Release Checklist &amp; Store Submission\\\"/><ac:link-body>Release Checklist &amp; Store Submission</ac:link-body></ac:link>. Scope covers Domino Delights (internal codename GoD, \\\"Game of Dominoes\\\"), Zombie Match 3D (listed as \\\"Puzzles vs Zombies\\\" until the July 2025 rename). Budget a few hours per title - the first five chapters alone run over an hour; if something fails, file the bug and block the release, don't hand-wave it.</p><ac:structured-macro ac:name=\\\"note\\\"><ac:rich-text-body><p><strong>Test the UPDATE path, not just a fresh install.</strong> The June 2025 progression losses were on <strong>Domino Delights</strong>, and nobody ever reproduced them - the leading hypothesis was the update path, which is why we test it. Every candidate must be installed <em>over</em> the last two shipped versions and checked for save/progression loss before we ship. If we only test fresh installs we will miss this entire class of bug again.</p></ac:rich-text-body></ac:structured-macro><h2>Recurring bug classes to always check</h2><p>These four have all bitten us in production. Treat them as non-negotiable checks on every pass, on real devices, not just the emulator.</p><ul><li><strong>Font / asset download stall on splash.</strong> In May 2025 Domino Delights froze at roughly 22% on the splash screen while pulling remote fonts and assets (ENG-2346, build 0.2.197). Watch the loading bar all the way to gameplay; test on a throttled/slow connection and with the app cache cleared.</li><li><strong>Lives &amp; scheduler regeneration.</strong> Confirm lives tick back up on the timer, refill correctly after device clock changes, and that offline time is credited without over-granting.</li><li><strong>Quest population &amp; reset.</strong> New quests populate on a fresh session, complete correctly, and roll over at the daily/weekly reset without duplicating or leaving empty slots.</li><li><strong>Save / progression integrity.</strong> Level, coins and unlocks survive an update, a force-quit and a re-launch. This is the one the June 2025 wipe belongs to.</li></ul><h2>Regression matrix</h2><p>Run each row on at least one iOS and one Android device. Both update rows are mandatory - N-1 and N-2 are the last two shipped store versions.</p><table><tbody><tr><th>Area</th><th>Fresh install</th><th>Update from N-1</th><th>Update from N-2</th><th>Notes</th></tr><tr><td>Splash / asset load</td><td>Pass</td><td>Pass</td><td>Pass</td><td>Watch for the ~22% stall (May 2025 Domino Delights)</td></tr><tr><td>Save / progression</td><td>Pass</td><td>Pass</td><td>Pass</td><td>June 2025 wipe never reproduced in-house; update path is the leading hypothesis</td></tr><tr><td>Lives &amp; scheduler</td><td>Pass</td><td>Pass</td><td>Pass</td><td>Test with device clock changes</td></tr><tr><td>Quests populate / reset</td><td>Pass</td><td>Pass</td><td>Pass</td><td>Check daily + weekly rollover</td></tr><tr><td>Purchase / restore</td><td>Pass</td><td>Pass</td><td>Pass</td><td>Sandbox account. NOTE: entitlements do <strong>not</strong> restore on reinstall and receipts are not validated server-side - see the per-title IAP row below. Check the purchase grants client-side, not that it restores.</td></tr></tbody></table><h3>If a check fails</h3><ul><li>File the bug with device, OS version, source build and repro steps, and link it here.</li><li>A failure in any UPDATE column blocks the release - fresh-install-only regressions do not pass the gate.</li><li>Player-facing symptoms (lost progress, stuck splash, missing lives) should be mirrored to the <ac:link><ri:page ri:space-key=\\\"OPS\\\" ri:content-title=\\\"Customer Support Playbook\\\"/><ac:link-body>Customer Support Playbook</ac:link-body></ac:link> so support has a known-issue note before it hits the store.</li></ul><ac:structured-macro ac:name=\\\"info\\\"><ac:rich-text-body><p>Once the matrix is green, tick the QA sign-off on the <ac:link><ri:page ri:space-key=\\\"OPS\\\" ri:content-title=\\\"Release Checklist &amp; Store Submission\\\"/><ac:link-body>Release Checklist &amp; Store Submission</ac:link-body></ac:link> before submitting to the stores.</p></ac:rich-text-body></ac:structured-macro><h2>Per-title regression matrix</h2><p>Each release candidate is walked through the full matrix below on at least one iOS and one Android device. A cell fails the gate if the observed behaviour deviates from the expected column; file the bug, mark the matrix red, and block the submission.</p>\\n<table><tbody><tr><th>Area</th><th>Check</th><th>Expected</th><th>Domino</th><th>ZM3D</th></tr>\\n<tr><td>First-time UX</td><td>Fresh install \\u2192 tutorial \\u2192 first win</td><td>No stall; tutorial completes; first level winnable</td><td>Yes</td><td>Yes</td></tr>\\n<tr><td>Font / localisation</td><td>Cold start on a fresh device (no cached font)</td><td>UI renders; no blank/boxed glyphs at ~22% load</td><td>Yes</td><td>Yes</td></tr>\\n<tr><td>Progression</td><td>Play 3 levels, force-quit, relaunch</td><td>Level and currency persist; no reset to level 1</td><td>Yes</td><td>Yes</td></tr>\\n<tr><td>Economy</td><td>Earn + spend coins, buy a booster</td><td>Balances reconcile; booster consumed once.</td><td>Yes</td><td>Yes</td></tr>\\n<tr><td>Live-ops</td><td>Enter the active event; complete a quest</td><td>Event loads; quest ticks; rewards granted</td><td>Yes</td><td>Yes</td></tr>\\n<tr><td>IAP</td><td>Sandbox purchase + restore</td><td>Item is granted client-side and purchases cannot be restored on reinstall. Receipts are effectively NOT validated: Domino Delights' Node backend exposes a <code>POST /validate-receipt</code> route, but it is dead scaffolding - <code>validateReceipt()</code> is never defined or imported, it logs undefined variables (<code>bodyRequest</code>, <code>packId</code>), and on success it grants nothing and returns <code>undefined</code>. Zombie Match 3D has no such route at all. Treat the route's existence as a trap: do not assume verification because an endpoint is there.</td><td>Yes</td><td>Yes</td></tr>\\n<tr><td>Ads</td><td>Watch a rewarded ad to completion</td><td>Reward granted once; no double-grant on retry</td><td>Yes</td><td>n/a</td></tr></tbody></table>\\n<h2>Known-issue regression cases</h2><p>These are the incidents that have bitten us in production; every one now has a permanent regression case so it cannot silently return. Reproduce the trigger, confirm the fix holds.</p>\\n<ac:structured-macro ac:name=\\\"warning\\\"><ac:rich-text-body><p><strong>Font-download stall.</strong> On a fresh device the dynamic font pack could fail to download, freezing the UI at ~22% of the <strong>splash-screen load</strong> (this is a position on the loading bar, not a share of players). Regression: install on a wiped device with a throttled connection; confirm a fallback font renders and load continues.</p></ac:rich-text-body></ac:structured-macro>\\n<ac:structured-macro ac:name=\\\"warning\\\"><ac:rich-text-body><p><strong>Progression reset.</strong> Returning players were once dropped back to level 1 with their player id unchanged in Metabase; the cause has not been established. Regression: migrate a save across a reinstall; reinstall with a LINKED Apple/Google account and confirm the server profile rehydrates. Quest-chest progression is known not to survive this (ENG-2114); unlinked guest accounts cannot be recovered at all.</p></ac:rich-text-body></ac:structured-macro>\\n<ac:structured-macro ac:name=\\\"warning\\\"><ac:rich-text-body><p><strong>Quest not resetting / not populating.</strong> A bad deploy left the daily quest board empty. Regression: roll the clock across a UTC day boundary; confirm quests repopulate and completion state resets.</p></ac:rich-text-body></ac:structured-macro>\\n<h2>Device lab</h2><p>The minimum matrix is one recent + one old device per platform. We keep a small physical lab plus a cloud device farm for the long tail.</p>\\n<table><tbody><tr><th>Platform</th><th>Recent</th><th>Old / min-spec</th><th>Tablet</th></tr>\\n<tr><td>iOS</td><td>current-gen iPhone, latest OS</td><td>iPhone SE-class, oldest supported OS</td><td>iPad (one gen)</td></tr>\\n<tr><td>Android</td><td>current-gen Samsung / Pixel</td><td>low-RAM device, oldest supported OS</td><td>one Android tablet</td></tr></tbody></table>\\n<h3>Sign-off</h3><p>QA posts the completed matrix in <code>#god-bugs (Domino Delights) or #zombie-bugs (ZM3D)</code> and links it from the release ticket. A build without a green matrix should not get submitted. In practice Friday is the usual release day and builds have shipped without a completed pass - QA has asked for a Friday freeze but it has not been adopted.</p>\", \"body_format\": \"storage\", \"status\": \"current\", \"version\": 2, \"author_id\": \"user_EMPLOYEE_0025\", \"created_at\": \"2024-04-15T10:11:23Z\", \"updated_at\": \"2025-08-04T10:00:00Z\"}"}

### [35] TOOL_RESULT (2098 chars)
{"result":"{\"mimeType\": \"text/markdown\", \"text\": \"# ZM submission rules\\nLeonard, Sep 23\\n\\nAfter the orange juice one on Sunday I want the gate tighter for every ZM store submission from now on, not just the Bug view in Linear.\\n\\nScope: every ZM ticket that's still open (any state but Done, Canceled or Duplicate), whatever team or label (ART or DES tickets with ZM in the title count too), plus everything that moved to Done after the last gate's builds were cut (Android #127 / iOS #40 on Sep 22). Release tickets, ZOM-359 and ZOM-475 stay out, that work is separate. From the next gate on, tickets filed while the gate is running get a row too, including the ones you file yourself.\\n\\nA ticket counts as fixed when Vincent or Brian saw it working on the candidate builds, or on prod if the fix is server side. A Done ticket for a bug nobody has re-checked yet also counts as fixed if its fix is actually in the candidate builds (or live on prod), noted as not re-checked. Done in Linear or a dev saying fixed isn't enough by itself, and if the latest check on the candidates still shows the bug, call it on how bad it is whatever the ticket says.\\n\\nBlocker = crash, freeze, player stuck or can't finish a level (like the orange juice one), lost progress / coins / lives (infinite-lives time included) / boosters / inventory items, or a purchase that doesn't deliver. Any other bug a player can see is a known issue, even if it doesn't change play, visual and sound glitches included. Art/VFX requests, requests for design, balance or tuning changes, analytics, tools, backend endpoints and new features aren't bugs, no call. The same goes for a ticket that only asks for new or updated art, animation, VFX, text style or UI layout (replacing an outdated model or icon with its new version included), even with a Bug label and even once it's Done, and for a ticket that doesn't say what goes wrong.\\n\\nBlockers go on the release ticket. If in doubt ask me before the go/no-go, not after.\\n\", \"id\": \"f_632c44650df44fe252ee59\", \"name\": \"ZM submission rules.docx\"}"}

### [37] TOOL_RESULT (960 chars)
{"result":"{\"mimeType\": \"text/markdown\", \"text\": \"# How ZM submission builds are cut\\nArthur, Sep 24\\n\\nSo nobody has to guess when gating:\\n\\n- Since the hotfix train ended on Sep 3, every store submission (the Match3D Android Submission and Match 3D iOS Submission jobs) is cut straight from main. The auth branch submission jobs are sign-in test builds, not store candidates.\\n- A submission has exactly what was on main when its job started (the Started line in #builds) and nothing that landed after, even if the build finishes an hour later. Don't go by the Changes block either: the iOS posts only ever print the newest change, Android prints the whole list.\\n- Backend is separate: match3d-backend changes reach players only when Douglas deploys the backend to prod, by hand (see the CI/CD page, CI stops at the build). Merged is not live.\\n\", \"id\": \"f_a038a576ca8db6955fae95\", \"name\": \"How ZM submission builds are cut.docx\"}"}

### [293] TOOL_RESULT (2242 chars)
{"result":"{\"id\": \"page_cicd_pipeline\", \"space_id\": \"space_ENG\", \"parent_id\": \"page_eng_home\", \"title\": \"CI/CD Pipeline & Branching\", \"body\": \"<p>How code gets from a pull request to production. In practice most work lands through long-lived feature branches rather than trunk-based development. Runtime gating is done with Firebase Remote Config (for example the A/B group assignment), not by branch strategy; deploys are rolling on ECS Fargate with health-check gating.</p><h2>Pipeline</h2><table><tbody><tr><th>Stage</th><th>What runs</th></tr><tr><td>PR</td><td>Lint, unit tests, build; required review before merge</td></tr><tr><td>Merge to main</td><td>The same <code>mix check</code> job runs again on push to main - there is no separate post-merge suite. <strong>CI stops here</strong> - the backend workflow (<code>match3d-backend/.github/workflows/check.yml</code>) runs compile and check on push/PR and has no deploy step.</td></tr><tr><td>Deploy</td><td><strong>Run by hand</strong>: rolling Fargate task-definition update via the <code>aws ecs update-service</code> command below; health checks gate promotion. The only CI-driven deploys in the org are the marketing site's S3 syncs (<code>website/.github/workflows/s3-deploy*.yml</code>).</td></tr><tr><td>Post-deploy</td><td>Smoke checks + alarm watch (see observability)</td></tr></tbody></table><ac:structured-macro ac:name=\\\"info\\\"><ac:rich-text-body><p>Client (Unity) builds are cut separately and go through staged store rollout - never 100% on day one. See the Ops release checklist.</p></ac:rich-text-body></ac:structured-macro><h2>Rollback</h2><ac:structured-macro ac:name=\\\"code\\\"><ac:plain-text-body><![CDATA[aws ecs update-service --cluster hg-prod --service game-api \\\\\\n  --task-definition game-api:317 --force-new-deployment]]></ac:plain-text-body></ac:structured-macro><p>Infra + deploy targets: <ac:link><ri:page ri:content-title=\\\"AWS Infrastructure Guide\\\"/><ac:link-body>AWS Infrastructure Guide</ac:link-body></ac:link>.</p>\", \"body_format\": \"storage\", \"status\": \"current\", \"version\": 2, \"author_id\": \"user_EMPLOYEE_0031\", \"created_at\": \"2023-12-01T10:24:23Z\", \"updated_at\": \"2025-03-18T03:10:01Z\"}"}

### [347] TOOL_RESULT (576 chars)
Hi Vincent,

Notes from today's two sessions on Puzzles vs Zombies, TestFlight build 50 (iPhone 14 on iOS 18.6 and iPhone SE on iOS 17.5), 6 players, about 45 minutes each.

1. Boss fight: for 3 of the 6 players, after the refill two or three single items dropped in with no matching items anywhere on the board. Nothing could clear them, the board never emptied and the boss timer ran out. Each of them lost a life and had to start the fight again.
2. Zombie Lane: two players asked what the cards are for. Not a bug, just feedback.

Best,
PERSON_9701_NAME
Cobalt Playtesting

### [502] ASSISTANT TEXT
The QA call on Android #135 / iOS #50 is **no-go**: 16 blockers. Everything is posted, and the call went up as a reply in Arthur's candidates thread, as Leonard asked on ZOM-570. [Slack reply](https://harmonygamesgroup.slack.com/archives/C08R27X0EAH/p1759302511000000?thread_ts=1759263900.484190&cid=C08R27X0EAH)

**What I did**
- **Gate register:** new tab "Sep 30 - Android #135 / iOS #50" in ZM3D Release Gate, in the same format as the Sep 22 tab. It has 133 rows: 16 blockers, 21 known issues, 10 fixed and 86 no call. Following Leonard's rules, that covers every open ZM ticket, plus the ones closed since the Sep 22 builds, plus the ones filed during this gate. ZOM-359, ZOM-475 and the release ticket are left out, as his rules say.
- **ZOM-570:** the 16 blockers are added to the description, each under its existing ticket.
- **New ticket, ZOM-581:** installing iOS #50 over the current store build (1.3.24) resets the save. A level 212 account with 18,400 coins came back at level 1 with 500 coins, and relaunching didn't fix it. This comes from Brian's update-path run, which he wrote up in a separate doc and only mentioned in a DM to you; nobody had filed it. It was the only runbook finding without a ticket. The other three update-path checks passed.

**Calls that go against what the team has been saying**
- **Brian's "regression matrix green" is wrong.** His matrix sheet only has the fresh-install columns filled in, and his update-path doc has the iOS failure above.
- **ZOM-435 (trophy path stuck) came back.** You saw it pass on #133, but Leonard got stuck on #135 (his comment is on the Trello tech-issues card).
- **ZOM-473 (Done) is still broken on #50.** Brian DM'd you in Turkish: he tapped past the endless end screen before the x2 and lost half the run's coins.
- **ZOM-569 (Done) is not fixed in these builds.** The fix went into main at 21:36Z, after both builds started (18:43Z and 18:45Z).
- **ZOM-567 (Done) is not live.** Arthur withdrew his approval on backend fix #36 because it pays out twice, so it stays on dev and Zombie Lane chests still don't land in the inventory on prod.
- **ZOM-562 moves from known issue to blocker.** Cobalt's playtest on TestFlight 50 had 3 of 6 players stuck with single items they couldn't match in the boss fight; each lost a life.

**Judgment calls to check**
- **ZOM-559 (Samsung sound/freeze):** called a blocker because Brian's latest check (on #133) shows a freeze and there's no fix on main. Nobody has re-tested it on #135 with a Samsung.
- **ZOM-443 and ZOM-438:** called known issues because a player could notice them, even though play isn't affected.

**Confirmed fixed:** ZOM-503, 506, 557, 561, 434, 558, 517, 564 and 429, plus ZOM-563, which is live on prod and which you confirmed with Douglas.
